# Result status registry

Audit receipts: [charter audit-01](topics/charter-unisolvence-20261031/audit-01.md) accepted its stated scopes; [MZ audit-01](topics/mz-constants/audit-01.md) and [audit-02](topics/mz-constants/audit-02.md) accepted the MZ scopes/response; [lattice audit-01](topics/lattice-unisolvence-20261031/audit-01.md) accepted all existing lattice labels/scopes. Acceptance does not close the separately registered OPEN targets or literature/novelty gaps. The [shared ledger](topics/padua-batch-20261031/ALLOCATIONS.md) preserves both final blocks and the authorized provisional lattice records.

Use repository-global IDs `PD-T001`, `PD-T002`, ... for positive or unresolved result statements and `PD-N001`, `PD-N002`, ... for scoped negative results. IDs are unique across tracks. Provisional IDs include a topic slug and agent, for example `PD-T?charter-codex-01`. Allocate final IDs at PR preparation after checking main and pending reservations. Record the mapping in [ID_CROSSWALK.md](ID_CROSSWALK.md); never renumber merged results.

Every mathematical result, including a distinct measured claim, has a [theory file](templates/THEORY.md) when produced and a row here at PR preparation. Published statements use `LIT-nnn` in the literature database and do not inherit a research proof status.

Split substantive proved results into their own files/IDs so they keep PROVED. Keep an original unresolved target registered with its true status and dependencies on the split results; record the split in [ID_CROSSWALK.md](ID_CROSSWALK.md). Minor routine steps do not contribute to aggregation. PARTIAL requires explicitly identified parts of the target's statement, some PROVED under split-off IDs and the remainder OPEN; supporting lemmas alone leave the unresolved target OPEN.

REJECTED is a separate disposition, not a proof status. A refuted registered claim must show `REJECTED: refuted (see PD-Nnnn)` with a link to its refuting result in the disposition column. Check this column before relying on any row: a rejected claim is not a usable dependency even if its earlier epistemic status remains as history. PD-N is reserved for scoped refuted/failed mechanisms; its status describes the negative statement. Equivalent or abandoned candidates stay as IDEA dispositions and do not create administrative PD-N rows.

Written mathematical statuses and dispositions are distinct. Claude audit-01 ACCEPTED the mathematical scope and all 13 status/disposition classifications at eb7bdcdf3a587dd49ea9d692b1d27c7af053fe64; [response-01](topics/charter-unisolvence-20261031/response-01.md) records the administrative resolutions. Final allocation authority: [shared batch ledger](topics/padua-batch-20261031/ALLOCATIONS.md); the [charter member ledger](topics/charter-unisolvence-20261031/ALLOCATIONS.md) preserves its historical reservation; provenance/splits: [crosswalk](ID_CROSSWALK.md). Every finite aggregate observation remains MEASURED; no general theorem depends on a random or floating sample. Check dispositions before relying on any result.

Original mathematical claims retain the exact accepted scopes of [Claude audit-01](topics/padua-argmax-alln-20261101/audit-01.md); [response-review-01](topics/padua-argmax-alln-20261101/response-review-01.md) accepts the record response and [audit-03](topics/padua-argmax-alln-20261101/audit-03-storage-repair.md) resolves CS1–CS3. This registration and the seven rehomed MEASURED records are accepted by the [distinct Astra final preparation audit](topics/padua-argmax-alln-20261101/prep-support/final-audit.md) under AC's 2026-11-03 instruction to finalize without further Claude rounds; actual-main integration has its [separate recheck](topics/padua-argmax-alln-20261101/prep-support/integration-audit.md). Earlier unchanged rows, including PD-T106's OPEN-target context and PD-N052's “actual norm PD-T114 OPEN” wording, describe their historical development state and are superseded by these current resolutions. PD-N052's failed majorants remain valid. The genuine one-dimensional PD-T027 target is now PARTIAL; its uniform gap and operator-identified limit are PROVED, with decimal value OPEN (see its row).

PD-T448 is PARTIAL: its exact benchmark Krawtchouk exponential-rate conjunct is PROVED under split-off PD-T509 with PD-T437; Krawtchouk prefactor/useful constants/radius and full Hahn rate/prefactor/useful constants/radius remain OPEN in the single declared metric. Supporting lower bounds, normality, measurements and scoped negatives do not enter that aggregation. The D1 mechanism questions remain OPEN supporting leads outside the formal target statement. PD-T449 remains OPEN; its supporting results do not form a PARTIAL decomposition. PD-T446/447 remain the OPEN copy/pair sub-target and parent bivariate-transfer target. Intrinsic sensitivity, finite accuracy gains and full-array backward stability remain distinct; no implemented algorithm has a general stability theorem here.
